What variables in the raw data is `input` *Previous trial outcome* derived from?\n   270→\n   271→i. Derived from the `Reward` behavior time series. Reward event timestamps are matched to behavior timestamps, and a binary `isreward` array marks timepoints where rewards occurred.\n   272→\n   273→ii.\n   274→```python\n   275→reward_amounts = behavior_data['Reward'].data[:][:T]\n   276→reward_times = behavior_data['Reward'].timestamps[:]\n   277→reward_time_indices = np.searchsorted(timestamps, reward_times)\n   278→isreward = np.zeros(T)\n   279→isreward[reward_time_indices] = 1\n   280→```\n   281→\n   282→iii. The `Reward` time series has its own timestamps (not aligned to the behavior sampling rate), so `searchsorted` is used to find the nearest timepoint. An assertion checks that the alignment error is within half a time bin.\n   283→\n   284→## 6-b. What processing is involved in computing `input` *Previous trial outcome*?\n   285→\n   286→i. For each trial, check whether any reward event occurred in the *previous* trial's time range. For the first trial, set to 0. The value is constant across all timepoints within a trial.\n   287→\n   288→ii.\n   289→```python\n   290→if trial == 0:\n   291→    input_curr[3,:] = 0\n   292→else:\n   293→    prev_reward_idx = np.arange(trial_start_idx[trial-1], trial_end_idx[trial-1], dtype=int)\n   294→    isprevreward = np.any(isreward[prev_reward_idx])\n   295→    input_curr[3,:] = int(isprevreward)\n   296→```\n   297→\n   298→iii. The instructions specify previous trial outcome as binary (omitted=0, rewarded=1). The code checks if any reward event fell within the previous trial's time boundaries.\n   299→\n   300→## 7-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Distance to reward zone* derived from?\n   301→\n   302→i. Derived from the `position` behavior time series and the reward zone location for the current trial. The reward zone boundaries come from the `reward_zone` behavior time series. This variable is non-zero when the mouse is in the reward zone. I combine this with the position of the mouse when this is non-zero to figure out the closest reward zone. To allow for some noise (of unknown source) and unrewarded tasks, I use the Viterbi algorithm to assign zones so that the rewarded positions match the known reward zones and it is uncommon to switch reward zone. \n   303→\n   304→ii. Assigning reward zone location for each trial: \n   305→- Before converting, the `survey()` function was used to try to understand all the data values, including whatever is going on with the `reward_zone` variable. It first computes the position of the min and max position of the mouse when `reward_zone` is non-zero:\n   306→```python\n   307→behavior_data = nwbfile.processing['behavior']['BehavioralTimeSeries'].time_series       \n   308→info['reward_zone_positions'] = np.full((ntrials, 2), np.nan)\n   309→position = behavior_data['position'].data[:]\n   310→for trial in range(ntrials):\n   311→    idx = np.arange(trial_start_idx[trial], trial_end_idx[trial], dtype=int)\n   312→    pos_trial = position[idx]\n   313→    reward_zone_trial = reward_zone[idx] > 0\n   314→    if not np.any(reward_zone_trial):\n   315→        continue\n   316→    info['reward_zone_positions'][trial] = [np.min(pos_trial[reward_zone_trial]), np.max(pos_trial[reward_zone_trial])]\n   317→```\n   318→The function `segment_reward_positions()` uses the Viterbi algorithm to assign reward zone labels so that the initial rewarded positions are close to the corresponding rewarded zone:\n   319→```python\n   320→reward_zone_dict = {'A': [80, 130],\n   321→                    'B': [200, 250],\n   322→                    'C': [320, 370]}\n   323→...\n   324→sigma = 10.0  # std dev for Gaussian emission on zone distance\n   325→zone_ranges = np.array([reward_zone_dict[z] for z in zone_names])  # (n_states, 2)\n   326→dist = np.maximum(zone_ranges[None, :, 0] - startpoints[valid, None],\n   327→                    startpoints[valid, None] - zone_ranges[None, :, 1]) # negative if inside the zone, positive if outside\n   328→dist = np.maximum(dist, 0)\n   329→log_emission[valid] = -0.5 * (dist / sigma) ** 2\n   330→```\n   331→It also encourages the reward zone label to not change often:\n   332→```python\n   333→pstay = .9\n   334→log_trans = np.full((n_states, n_states), np.log((1 - pstay) / (n_states - 1)))\n   335→np.fill_diagonal(log_trans, np.log(pstay))\n   336→```\n   337→\n   338→iii. The paper defines reward zones A, B, C at specific position ranges. These same ranges could also be found, with some translation, in the code (required mapping X -> A, etc.). I plotted the position of the mouse when `reward_zone` was positive (note that the value was between 0 and 6 I think) to determine that the Viterbi approach would likely work, and that the start of the rewarded position was the least noisy variable to use. The class fractions were approximately 1/3 after this, so it seemed correct. \n   339→\n   340→## 7-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Distance to reward zone*?\n   341→\n   342→i. For each timepoint, compute the signed distance from the animal's position to the nearest edge of the reward zone. Distance is 0 when inside the zone, negative when before the zone, and positive when past it.\n   343→\n   344→ii.\n   345→```python\n   346→def compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position_curr, reward_zone_curr):\n   347→    ntimepoints = len(position_curr)\n   348→    reward_zone_range = reward_zone_dict[reward_zone_curr]\n   349→    distance_to_reward_zone = np.zeros(ntimepoints)\n   350→    idx_before_zone = position_curr < reward_zone_range[0]\n   351→    distance_to_reward_zone[idx_before_zone] = position_curr[idx_before_zone] - reward_zone_range[0]\n   352→    idx_after_zone = position_curr > reward_zone_range[1]\n   353→    distance_to_reward_zone[idx_after_zone] = position_curr[idx_after_zone] - reward_zone_range[1]\n   354→    return distance_to_reward_zone\n   355→```\n   356→\n   357→iii. This matches the paper's concept of distance relative to the reward zone. The reward zone boundaries are from `reward_zone_dict`, inspired by the reference code `Sosa_et_al_2024/src/reward_relative/behavior.py`.\n   358→\n   359→## 7-c. How is `output` *Distance to reward zone* thresholded into categories?\n   360→\n   361→i. The continuous distance is discretized into 7 bins using `np.digitize` with bin edges `[-inf, -50, -10, 0, 1e-6, 10, 50, inf]`.\n   362→\n   363→ii.\n   364→```python\n   365→distance_to_reward_zone_bins = [-np.inf, -50, -10, 0, 1e-6, 10, 50, np.inf]\n   366→...\n   367→output_curr[0] = np.digitize(distance_to_reward_zone, distance_to_reward_zone_bins) - 1\n   368→```\n   369→\n   370→iii. The bin edges match the instructions. The `1e-6` boundary separates exactly 0 (in the zone boundary) from slightly positive. The `-1` corrects `np.digitize` to be 0-indexed.\n   371→\n   372→## 7-d. How is `output` *Distance to reward zone* aligned with the neural data?\n   373→\n   374→i. The position data and neural data share the same time indices within each trial, so no additional alignment is needed.\n   375→\n   376→ii.\n   377→```python\n   378→idx = np.arange(trial_start_idx[trial], trial_end_idx[trial], dtype=int)\n   379→spike_data_curr = spike_data[idx]\n   380→position_curr = position[idx]\n   381→```\n   382→\n   383→iii. Both neural and behavioral data use the same time indexing (verified by timestamp assertions earlier in the code), so indexing by the same `idx` array ensures alignment.\n   384→\n   385→## 8-a. How are minor mistakes in the data, e.g. missing data, handled?\n   515→\n   516→i. Several cases are handled:\n   517→- **Neural/behavior length mismatch**: If the number of neural time bins differs from behavior timestamps, data is cropped to the minimum of the two with a warning printed.\n   518→- **Short trials**: Trials with fewer than 50 timepoints (`min_ntimepoints`) are skipped with a warning.\n   519→- **Missing reward zone data**: Trials where `reward_zone` is never active (all zeros) get NaN positions, and the Viterbi algorithm handles these via uniform emission (assignment driven by neighboring trials' transitions).\n   520→- **Reward timestamp alignment**: An assertion verifies reward times align within half a time bin of behavior timestamps.\n   521→\n   522→ii.\n   523→```python\n   524→if T != len(timestamps):\n   525→    print(f\"Warning: number of neural time bins ({T}) does not match number of behavior timestamps ({len(timestamps)}), cropping to the minimum of the two\")\n   526→    T = min(T, len(timestamps))\n   527→    spike_data = spike_data[:T]\n   528→    timestamps = timestamps[:T]\n   529→...\n   530→if idx.sum() < min_ntimepoints:\n   531→    print(f\"Skipping trial {trial}...\")\n   532→    istrial[trial] = False\n   533→    continue\n   534→```\n   535→\n   536→iii. These are defensive checks found during data exploration. The neural/behavior mismatch occurs in a small number of sessions.\n   537→\n   538→## 13-a. What are the most time-consuming steps of the code?\n   539→\n   540→i. Neural data is from the `Deconvolved` field\n\nii. \n```python\ndeconvolved_data = nwbfile.processing[\"ophys\"][\"Deconvolved\"]\nfor plane_data in deconvolved_data.roi_response_series.values():\n    spike_data_curr = plane_data.data[:]\n```\n\niii. The deconvolved data are already processed. The neural data rate is the same for all the recordings, once we account for the multi-plane recordings. \n\n## 2-c. Neural data is filtered based on the `iscell` variable. \n\nii. \n```python\ndeconvolved_data = nwbfile.processing[\"ophys\"][\"Deconvolved\"]\nspike_data = []\nnplanes = len(deconvolved_data.roi_response_series)\nfor plane_data in deconvolved_data.roi_response_series.values():\n    roi_data = plane_data.rois[:]\n    iscell = np.array(roi_data['iscell'].to_list())[:,0] == 1\n    spike_data_curr = plane_data.data[:]\n    assert spike_data_curr.shape[1] == iscell.shape[0], f\"Number of neurons in data ({spike_data_curr.shape[1]}) does not match number of iscell entries ({iscell.shape[0]})\"\n    spike_data.append(spike_data_curr[:,iscell])\n```\n\niii. Derived from the `Reward` behavior time series. Reward event timestamps are matched to behavior timestamps, and a binary `isreward` array marks timepoints where rewards occurred.\n\nii.\n```python\nreward_amounts = behavior_data['Reward'].data[:][:T]\nreward_times = behavior_data['Reward'].timestamps[:]\nreward_time_indices = np.searchsorted(timestamps, reward_times)\nisreward = np.zeros(T)\nisreward[reward_time_indices] = 1\n```\n\niii. An assertion checks that the alignment error is within half a time bin.\n\n## 6-b. What processing is involved in computing `input` *Previous trial outcome*?\n\ni. For each trial, check whether any reward event occurred in the *previous* trial's time range. For the first trial, set to 0. The value is constant across all timepoints within a trial.\n\nii.\n```python\nif trial == 0:\n    input_curr[3,:] = 0\nelse:\n    prev_reward_idx = np.arange(trial_start_idx[trial-1], trial_end_idx[trial-1], dtype=int)\n    isprevreward = np.any(isreward[prev_reward_idx])\n    input_curr[3,:] = int(isprevreward)\n```\n\niii. The instructions specify previous trial outcome as binary (omitted=0, rewarded=1). The code checks if any reward event fell within the previous trial's time boundaries.\n\n## 7-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Distance to reward zone* derived from?\n\ni. Derived from the `position` behavior time series and the reward zone location for the current trial. The reward zone boundaries come from the `reward_zone` behavior time series. This variable is non-zero when the mouse is in the reward zone. I combine this with the position of the mouse when this is non-zero to figure out the closest reward zone. To allow for some noise (of unknown source) and unrewarded tasks, I use the Viterbi algorithm to assign zones so that the rewarded positions match the known reward zones and it is uncommon to switch reward zone. \n\nii. Assigning reward zone location for each trial: \n- Before converting, the `survey()` function was used to try to understand all the data values, including whatever is going on with the `reward_zone` variable. It first computes the position of the min and max position of the mouse when `reward_zone` is non-zero:\n```python\nbehavior_data = nwbfile.processing['behavior']['BehavioralTimeSeries'].time_series       \ninfo['reward_zone_positions'] = np.full((ntrials, 2), np.nan)\nposition = behavior_data['position'].data[:]\nfor trial in range(ntrials):\n    idx = np.arange(trial_start_idx[trial], trial_end_idx[trial], dtype=int)\n    pos_trial = position[idx]\n    reward_zone_trial = reward_zone[idx] > 0\n    if not np.any(reward_zone_trial):\n        continue\n    info['reward_zone_positions'][trial] = [np.min(pos_trial[reward_zone_trial]), np.max(pos_trial[reward_zone_trial])]\n```\nThe function `segment_reward_positions()` uses the Viterbi algorithm to assign reward zone labels so that the initial rewarded positions are close to the corresponding rewarded zone:\n```python\nreward_zone_dict = {'A': [80, 130],\n                    'B': [200, 250],\n                    'C': [320, 370]}\n...\nsigma = 10.0  # std dev for Gaussian emission on zone distance\nzone_ranges = np.array([reward_zone_dict[z] for z in zone_names])  # (n_states, 2)\ndist = np.maximum(zone_ranges[None, :, 0] - startpoints[valid, None],\n                    startpoints[valid, None] - zone_ranges[None, :, 1]) # negative if inside the zone, positive if outside\ndist = np.maximum(dist, 0)\nlog_emission[valid] = -0.5 * (dist / sigma) ** 2\n```\nIt also encourages the reward zone label to not change often:\n```python\npstay = .9\nlog_trans = np.full((n_states, n_states), np.log((1 - pstay) / (n_states - 1)))\nnp.fill_diagonal(log_trans, np.log(pstay))\n```\n\niii. The paper defines reward zones A, B, C at specific position ranges. These same ranges could also be found, with some translation, in the code (required mapping X -> A, etc.). I plotted the position of the mouse when `reward_zone` was positive (note that the value was between 0 and 6 I think) to determine that the Viterbi approach would likely work, and that the start of the rewarded position was the least noisy variable to use. The class fractions were approximately 1/3 after this, so it seemed correct. \n\n## 7-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Distance to reward zone*?\n\ni. For each timepoint, compute the signed distance from the animal's position to the nearest edge of the reward zone. Distance is 0 when inside the zone, negative when before the zone, and positive when past it.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position_curr, reward_zone_curr):\n    ntimepoints = len(position_curr)\n    reward_zone_range = reward_zone_dict[reward_zone_curr]\n    distance_to_reward_zone = np.zeros(ntimepoints)\n    idx_before_zone = position_curr < reward_zone_range[0]\n    distance_to_reward_zone[idx_before_zone] = position_curr[idx_before_zone] - reward_zone_range[0]\n    idx_after_zone = position_curr > reward_zone_range[1]\n    distance_to_reward_zone[idx_after_zone] = position_curr[idx_after_zone] - reward_zone_range[1]\n    return distance_to_reward_zone\n```\n\niii. This matches the paper's concept of distance relative to the reward zone. The reward zone boundaries are from `reward_zone_dict`, inspired by the reference code `Sosa_et_al_2024/src/reward_relative/behavior.py`.\n\n## 7-c. How is `output` *Distance to reward zone* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. The continuous distance is discretized into 7 bins using `np.digitize` with bin edges `[-inf, -50, -10, 0, 1e-6, 10, 50, inf]`.\n\nii.\n```python\ndistance_to_reward_zone_bins = [-np.inf, -50, -10, 0, 1e-6, 10, 50, np.inf]\n...\noutput_curr[0] = np.digitize(distance_to_reward_zone, distance_to_reward_zone_bins) - 1\n```\n\niii. The bin edges match the instructions. The `1e-6` boundary separates exactly 0 (in the zone boundary) from slightly positive. The `-1` corrects `np.digitize` to be 0-indexed.\n\n## 7-d. How are minor mistakes in the data, e.g. missing data, handled?\n\ni. Several cases are handled:\n- **Neural/behavior length mismatch**: If the number of neural time bins differs from behavior timestamps, data is cropped to the minimum of the two with a warning printed.\n- **Short trials**: Trials with fewer than 50 timepoints (`min_ntimepoints`) are skipped with a warning.\n- **Missing reward zone data**: Trials where `reward_zone` is never active (all zeros) get NaN positions, and the Viterbi algorithm handles these via uniform emission (assignment driven by neighboring trials' transitions).\n- **Reward timestamp alignment**: An assertion verifies reward times align within half a time bin of behavior timestamps.\n\nii.\n```python\nif T != len(timestamps):\n    print(f\"Warning: number of neural time bins ({T}) does not match number of behavior timestamps ({len(timestamps)}), cropping to the minimum of the two\")\n    T = min(T, len(timestamps))\n    spike_data = spike_data[:T]\n    timestamps = timestamps[:T]\n...\nif idx.sum() < min_ntimepoints:\n    print(f\"Skipping trial {trial}...\")\n    istrial[trial] = False\n    continue\n```\n\niii. These are defensive checks found during data exploration. The neural/behavior mismatch occurs in a small number of sessions.\n\n## 13-a. What are the most time-consuming steps of the code?\n\ni. Some operations like `np.digitize` and `compute_distance_to_reward_zone` could theoretically be applied to the full session arrays before splitting into trials, but the variable trial lengths make this awkward. The Viterbi inner loop (lines 503-507) iterates over states, which could be vectorized with matrix operations.\n\nii. N/A\n\niii. The per-trial loop is the natural structure given variable-length trials. Vectorizing would require padding or masking.\n\n## 13-c. What processing does the code repeat multiple times?\n\ni. The survey step and the conversion step both load every NWB file and read the same data arrays (neural, behavior). The survey reads neural data to count neurons and check rates, then the conversion reads it again. The `reward_zone_positions` are computed in the survey and then used in the conversion, but other data (position, speed, lick, etc.) is read twice. \n\nii. N/A\n\niii. I don't think these could be run at the same time, as it would require too much RAM. The Viterbi output created during surveying is required to process the data. It was convenient to run the survey before deciding how to handle the conversion of the data. \n\n## 13-d. What unnecessary processing does the code do that is discarded in downstream analyses?\n\ni. Parse \"to\" side\n    86→        to_parts = parts[to_idx+1:]\n    87→\n    88→        # Extract zone letter and env from each side\n    89→        from_zone, from_env = _parse_zone_and_env(from_parts)\n    90→        to_zone, to_env = _parse_zone_and_env(to_parts)\n    91→\n    92→        labels[:change_trial] = from_zone\n    93→        labels[change_trial:] = to_zone\n    94→        coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT[from_zone]\n    95→        coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT[to_zone]\n    96→        env_per_trial[:change_trial] = from_env\n    97→        env_per_trial[change_trial:] = to_env\n    98→    else:\n    99→        # Single zone session: Env1_LocationA or similar\n   100→        parts = scene.split('_')\n   101→        zone, env = _parse_zone_and_env(parts)\n   102→        labels[:] = zone\n   103→        coords[:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT[zone]\n   104→        env_per_trial[:] = env\n   105→\n   106→    return labels, coords, env_per_trial\n   107→\n   108→\n   109→def _parse_zone_and_env(parts):\n   110→    \"\"\"Parse zone letter and env number from scene name parts.\n   111→\n   112→    Examples:\n   113→        ['Env1', 'LocationA'] -> ('A', 0)\n   114→        ['Env2', 'B'] -> ('B', 1)\n   115→        ['Env1', 'LocationB'] -> ('B', 0)\n   116→    \"\"\"\n   117→    env = 0\n   118→    zone = 'A'\n   119→\n   120→    for p in parts:\n   121→        if p.startswith('Env'):\n   122→            env_num = int(p.replace('Env', ''))\n   123→            env = env_num - 1  # 0-indexed\n   124→        elif p.startswith('Location'):\n   125→            zone = p.replace('Location', '')\n   126→        elif len(p) == 1 and p in 'ABC':\n   127→            zone = p\n   128→\n   129→    return zone, env\n   130→\n   131→\n   132→def compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, rz_start, rz_end):\n   133→    \"\"\"Compute signed distance from position to reward zone.\n   134→\n   135→    Negative = before zone (position < zone start)\n   136→    Positive = after zone (position > zone end)\n   137→    Zero = inside zone\n   138→    \"\"\"\n   139→    dist = np.zeros_like(position)\n   140→    before = position < rz_start\n   141→    after = position > rz_end\n   142→    inside = ~before & ~after\n   143→\n   144→    dist[before] = position[before] - rz_start  # negative\n   145→    dist[after] = position[after] - rz_end       # positive\n   146→    dist[inside] = 0.0\n   147→\n   148→    return dist\n   149→\n   150→\n   151→def discretize_distance(distance):\n   152→    \"\"\"Discretize distance to reward zone into 7 bins.\n   153→    0: < -50 cm\n   154→    1: -50 to -10 cm\n   155→    2: -10 cm to < 0 cm\n   156→    3: 0 cm (in zone)\n   157→    4: >0 cm to +10 cm\n   158→    5: +10 to +50 cm\n   159→    6: > +50 cm\n   160→    \"\"\"\n   161→    bins = np.zeros(len(distance), dtype=np.int64)\n   162→    bins[distance < -50] = 0\n   163→    bins[(distance >= -50) & (distance < -10)] = 1\n   164→    bins[(distance >= -10) & (distance < 0)] = 2\n   165→    bins[distance == 0] = 3\n   166→    bins[(distance > 0) & (distance <= 10)] = 4\n   167→    bins[(distance > 10) & (distance <= 50)] = 5\n   168→    bins[distance > 50] = 6\n   169→    return bins\n   170→\n   171→\n   172→def discretize_position(position):\n   173→    \"\"\"Discretize absolute position into 5 equal bins (0-90, 90-180, 180-270, 270-360, 360-450).\"\"\"\n   174→    bins = np.clip(np.floor(position / 90.0).astype(np.int64), 0, 4)\n   175→    return bins\n   176→\n   177→\n   178→def discretize_speed(speed):\n   179→    \"\"\"Discretize speed into 5 bins.\n   180→    0: < 2 cm/s\n   181→    1: 2-10 cm/s\n   182→    2: 10-20 cm/s\n   183→    3: 20-40 cm/s\n   184→    4: > 40 cm/s\n   185→    \"\"\"\n   186→    bins = np.zeros(len(speed), dtype=np.int64)\n   187→    bins[speed < 2] = 0\n   188→    bins[(speed >= 2) & (speed < 10)] = 1\n   189→    bins[(speed >= 10) & (speed < 20)] = 2\n   190→    bins[(speed >= 20) & (speed < 40)] = 3\n   191→    bins[speed >= 40] = 4\n   192→    return bins\n   193→\n   194→\n   195→def process_session(filepath, show_processing=False, session_idx=0):\n   196→    \"\"\"Process a single NWB file and return trial-segmented data.\n   197→\n   198→    Returns dict with keys: neural, input, output, n_neurons, subject,\n   199→           scene, n_trials, brain_region, or None if session should be skipped.\n   200→    \"\"\"\n   201→    t0 = time.time()\n   202→\n   203→    with h5py.File(filepath, 'r') as f:\n   204→        # === Metadata ===\n   205→        subject_id = f['general/subject/subject_id'][()].decode()\n   206→        identifier = f['identifier'][()].decode()\n   207→        scene = parse_scene(identifier)\n   208→        imaging_rate = f['general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/imaging_rate'][()]\n   209→        location = f['general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/location'][()].decode()\n   210→\n   211→        # === Neural data (handle multi-plane) ===\n   212→        iscell = f['processing/ophys/ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/iscell'][:]  # (n_ROIs_total, 2)\n   213→        planeIdx = f['processing/ophys/ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/planeIdx'][:]\n   214→\n   215→        # Determine available planes\n   216→        deconv_group = f['processing/ophys/Deconvolved']\n   217→        planes = sorted(deconv_group.keys())  # e.g. ['plane0'] or ['plane0', 'plane1']\n   218→\n   219→        # Load and concatenate data from all planes\n   220→        deconv_list = []\n   221→        flu_list = []\n   222→        neu_list = []\n   223→        plane_cell_offset = 0\n   224→        plane_offsets = {}\n   225→        for plane in planes:\n   226→            plane_num = int(plane.replace('plane', ''))\n   227→            plane_mask = planeIdx == plane_num\n   228→            plane_offsets[plane_num] = np.where(plane_mask)[0]\n   229→\n   230→            d = f[f'processing/ophys/Deconvolved/{plane}/data'][:]  # (n_timepoints, n_rois_plane)\n   231→            fl = f[f'processing/ophys/Fluorescence/{plane}/data'][:]\n   232→            ne = f[f'processing/ophys/Neuropil/{plane}/data'][:]\n   233→            deconv_list.append(d)\n   234→            flu_list.append(fl)\n   235→            neu_list.append(ne)\n   236→\n   237→        # Concatenate across planes: (n_timepoints, n_rois_total_in_data)\n   238→        deconv = np.concatenate(deconv_list, axis=1)\n   239→        fluorescence = np.concatenate(flu_list, axis=1)\n   240→        neuropil_data = np.concatenate(neu_list, axis=1)\n   241→\n   242→        # Build mapping from concatenated column index to iscell row index\n   243→        concat_to_iscell = []\n   244→        for plane in planes:\n   245→            plane_num = int(plane.replace('plane', ''))\n   246→            concat_to_iscell.append(plane_offsets[plane_num])\n   247→        concat_to_iscell = np.concatenate(concat_to_iscell)\n   248→\n   249→        # === Behavior ===\n   250→        position = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/position/data'][:]\n   251→        speed = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/speed/data'][:]\n   252→        lick = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/lick/data'][:]\n   253→        environment = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/environment/data'][:]\n   254→        trial_start = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/trial_start/data'][:]\n   255→        teleport_sig = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/teleport/data'][:]\n   256→        behav_timestamps = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/position/timestamps'][:]\n   257→\n   258→        # Reward events (sparse)\n   259→        reward_timestamps = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/Reward/timestamps'][:]\n   260→\n   261→    n_timepoints, n_rois_concat = deconv.shape\n   262→\n   263→    # === Align behavior and neural data lengths ===\n   264→    n_behav = len(position)\n   265→    if n_timepoints != n_behav:\n   266→        min_len = min(n_timepoints, n_behav)\n   267→        print(f\"  NOTE: Aligned neural ({n_timepoints}) and behavior ({n_behav}) to {min_len} timepoints\")\n   268→        deconv = deconv[:min_len]\n   269→        fluorescence = fluorescence[:min_len]\n   270→        neuropil_data = neuropil_data[:min_len]\n   271→        position = position[:min_len]\n   272→        speed = speed[:min_len]\n   273→        lick = lick[:min_len]\n   274→        environment = environment[:min_len]\n   275→        trial_start = trial_start[:min_len]\n   276→        teleport_sig = teleport_sig[:min_len]\n   277→        behav_timestamps = behav_timestamps[:min_len]\n   278→        n_timepoints = min_len\n   279→\n   280→    # === Cell filtering ===\n   281→    # Step 1: iscell filter - map to concatenated indices\n   282→    cell_mask_concat = np.array([iscell[concat_to_iscell[i], 0] == 1 for i in range(n_rois_concat)])\n   283→\n   284→    # Step 2: Interneuron exclusion - compute dF/F correlation with speed\n   285→    f_corrected = fluorescence - 0.7 * neuropil_data\n   286→    f_median = np.median(f_corrected, axis=0, keepdims=True)\n   287→    f_median[f_median == 0] = 1\n   288→    dff_simple = (f_corrected - f_median) / np.abs(f_median)\n   289→\n   290→    valid_mask = (speed > 0) & (position >= 0) & ~np.isnan(speed)\n   291→    interneuron_mask = np.zeros(n_rois_concat, dtype=bool)\n   292→\n   293→    if valid_mask.sum() > 100:\n   294→        speed_valid = speed[valid_mask]\n   295→        accepted_cols = np.where(cell_mask_concat)[0]\n   296→        if len(accepted_cols) > 0:\n   297→            # Vectorized correlation: z-score speed and dff, then dot product\n   298→            speed_z = (speed_valid - speed_valid.mean()) / (speed_valid.std() + 1e-10)\n   299→            dff_accepted = dff_simple[valid_mask][:, accepted_cols]\n   300→            dff_mean = dff_accepted.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)\n   301→            dff_std = dff_accepted.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)\n   302→            dff_std[dff_std == 0] = 1e-10\n   303→            dff_z = (dff_accepted - dff_mean) / dff_std\n   304→            corrs = (speed_z @ dff_z) / len(speed_z)\n   305→            for i, col_idx in enumerate(accepted_cols):\n   306→                if corrs[i] > 0.5:\n   307→                    interneuron_mask[col_idx] = True\n   308→\n   309→    # Final cell mask\n   310→    final_cell_mask = cell_mask_concat & ~interneuron_mask\n   311→    n_accepted = cell_mask_concat.sum()\n   312→    n_interneurons = (cell_mask_concat & interneuron_mask).sum()\n   313→    n_final = final_cell_mask.sum()\n   314→\n   315→    # Filter neural data to accepted cells\n   316→    neural_all = deconv[:, final_cell_mask].T  # (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\n   317→    neural_all = neural_all.astype(np.float32)\n   318→\n   319→    # === Trial boundaries ===\n   320→    trial_start_inds = np.where(trial_start > 0)[0]\n   321→    teleport_inds = np.where(teleport_sig > 0)[0]\n   322→    n_trials = len(trial_start_inds)\n   323→\n   324→    if n_trials < 2:\n   325→        print(f\"  WARNING: Only {n_trials} trials, skipping session\")\n   326→        return None\n   327→\n   328→    # Match teleport to trial start (ensure same count)\n   329→    if len(teleport_inds) != n_trials:\n   330→        # Try to match: each teleport should come after corresponding trial start\n   331→        matched_teleports = []\n   332→        for ts in trial_start_inds:\n   333→            tp_after = teleport_inds[teleport_inds > ts]\n   334→            if len(tp_after) > 0:\n   335→                matched_teleports.append(tp_after[0])\n   336→        teleport_inds = np.array(matched_teleports)\n   337→        n_trials = min(n_trials, len(teleport_inds))\n   338→        trial_start_inds = trial_start_inds[:n_trials]\n   339→\n   340→    # === Reward zone labels ===\n   341→    rz_labels, rz_coords, env_per_trial = get_reward_zone_labels(scene, n_trials)\n   342→\n   343→    # === Map reward events to trial indices ===\n   344→    # Create frame-aligned reward signal\n   345→    reward_frames = np.zeros(n_timepoints, dtype=np.float32)\n   346→    for rt in reward_timestamps:\n   347→        frame_idx = np.argmin(np.abs(behav_timestamps - rt))\n   348→        reward_frames[frame_idx] = 1.0\n   349→\n   350→    # Determine reward outcome per trial\n   351→    trial_rewarded = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=np.int64)\n   352→    for t in range(n_trials):\n   353→        si = trial_start_inds[t]\n   354→        ei = teleport_inds[t]\n   355→        if np.any(reward_frames[si:ei] > 0):\n   356→            trial_rewarded[t] = 1\n   357→\n   358→    # === Environment type per trial ===\n   359→    # Use env_per_trial from scene parsing (more reliable for cross-env switches)\n   360→    # Fall back to NWB environment field where scene doesn't specify\n   361→    trial_env = env_per_trial.copy()\n   362→\n   363→    # === Process licks ===\n   364→    # Following reference code: lick is cumulative per frame, set >1 to 1\n   365→    lick_binary = lick.copy()\n   366→    lick_binary[lick_binary > 0] = 1\n   367→    # Lick sensor error correction per trial (>30% frames with count>2 -> NaN)\n   368→    for t in range(n_trials):\n   369→        si = trial_start_inds[t]\n   370→        ei = teleport_inds[t]\n   371→        trial_lick = lick[si:ei]\n   372→        if len(trial_lick) > 0 and (np.sum(trial_lick > 2) / len(trial_lick)) > 0.30:\n   373→            lick_binary[si:ei] = 0  # Set to 0 instead of NaN for cleaner output\n   374→\n   375→    # === Previous trial outcome (lagged) ===\n   376→    prev_outcome = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=np.int64)\n   377→    for t in range(1, n_trials):\n   378→        prev_outcome[t] = trial_rewarded[t - 1]\n   379→    # Trial 0: no previous, use 0 (omission)\n   380→\n   381→    # === Segment into trials ===\n   382→    neural_trials = []\n   383→    input_trials = []\n   384→    output_trials = []\n   385→\n   386→    frame_time = 1.0 / imaging_rate  # seconds per frame\n   387→\n   388→    for t in range(n_trials):\n   389→        si = trial_start_inds[t]\n   390→        ei = teleport_inds[t]\n   391→        n_tp = ei - si\n   392→\n   393→        if n_tp < 2:\n   394→            continue\n   395→\n   396→        # Neural: (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\n   397→        trial_neural = neural_all[:, si:ei].copy()\n   398→        # Replace NaN with 0 in neural data\n   399→        trial_neural = np.nan_to_num(trial_neural, nan=0.0)\n   400→\n   401→        # === Inputs ===\n   402→        # Time from start of trial (seconds)\n   403→        time_from_start = np.arange(n_tp, dtype=np.float32) * frame_time\n   404→\n   405→        # Per-trial inputs\n   406→        env_type = np.float32(trial_env[t])\n   407→        trial_num = np.float32(t)\n   408→        prev_out = np.float32(prev_outcome[t])\n   409→\n   410→        # input shape: (n_input, n_timepoints) for time-varying, or (n_input,) for per-trial\n   411→        # We have 1 time-varying + 3 per-trial\n   412→        # Time-varying: time_from_start (1, n_tp)\n   413→        # Per-trial: env_type, trial_num, prev_outcome (3,)\n   414→        trial_input_tv = time_from_start.reshape(1, -1)  # (1, n_tp)\n   415→        trial_input_pt = np.array([env_type, trial_num, prev_out], dtype=np.float32)  # (3,)\n   416→        # Combine: first dim is time-varying, rest are per-trial\n   417→        # Per spec: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input,)\n   418→        # We need to combine: expand per-trial to time-varying by repeating\n   419→        trial_input = np.vstack([\n   420→            trial_input_tv,\n   421→            np.full((1, n_tp), env_type, dtype=np.float32),\n   422→            np.full((1, n_tp), trial_num, dtype=np.float32),\n   423→            np.full((1, n_tp), prev_out, dtype=np.float32),\n   424→        ])  # (4, n_tp)\n   425→\n   426→        # === Outputs ===\n   427→        trial_pos = position[si:ei]\n   428→        trial_speed = speed[si:ei]\n   429→        trial_lick = lick_binary[si:ei]\n   430→\n   431→        # Distance to reward zone\n   432→        rz_start = rz_coords[t, 0]\n   433→        rz_end = rz_coords[t, 1]\n   434→        signed_dist = compute_distance_to_reward_zone(trial_pos, rz_start, rz_end)\n   435→        dist_bins = discretize_distance(signed_dist)\n   436→\n   437→        # Absolute position bins\n   438→        pos_bins = discretize_position(trial_pos)\n   439→\n   440→        # Speed bins\n   441→        speed_bins = discretize_speed(np.abs(trial_speed))  # use absolute speed\n   442→\n   443→        # Lick binary\n   444→        lick_out = (trial_lick > 0).astype(np.int64)\n   445→\n   446→        # Reward zone location (per-trial): A=0, B=1, C=2\n   447→        zone_map = {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'C': 2}\n   448→        rz_loc = zone_map.get(rz_labels[t], 0)\n   449→\n   450→        # Reward outcome (per-trial): 0=no, 1=yes\n   451→        rew_outcome = int(trial_rewarded[t])\n   452→\n   453→        # Combine outputs: time-varying + per-trial\n   454→        # Time-varying: dist_bins, pos_bins, speed_bins, lick (4, n_tp)\n   455→        # Per-trial: rz_loc, rew_outcome (2,)\n   456→        trial_output = np.vstack([\n   457→            dist_bins.reshape(1, -1),\n   458→            pos_bins.reshape(1, -1),\n   459→            speed_bins.reshape(1, -1),\n   460→            lick_out.reshape(1, -1),\n   461→            np.full((1, n_tp), rz_loc, dtype=np.int64),\n   462→            np.full((1, n_tp), rew_outcome, dtype=np.int64),\n   463→        ])  # (6, n_tp)\n   464→\n   465→        neural_trials.append(trial_neural.astype(np.float32))\n   466→        input_trials.append(trial_input.astype(np.float32))\n   467→        output_trials.append(trial_output.astype(np.int64))\n   468→\n   469→    t1 = time.time()\n   470→    print(f\"  {os.path.basename(filepath)}: {n_final} neurons ({n_accepted} accepted, {n_interneurons} interneurons removed), \"\n   471→          f\"{len(neural_trials)} trials, scene={scene}, env={np.unique(trial_env)}, \"\n   472→          f\"reward_rate={trial_rewarded.mean():.2f}, time={t1-t0:.1f}s\")\n   473→\n   474→    if show_processing and session_idx < 2:\n   475→        plot_processing(filepath, neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n   476→                       position, speed, lick_binary, trial_start_inds, teleport_inds,\n   477→                       rz_coords, rz_labels, trial_rewarded, behav_timestamps,\n   478→                       subject_id, scene, session_idx)\n   479→\n   480→    return {\n   481→        'neural': neural_trials,\n   482→        'input': input_trials,\n   483→        'output': output_trials,\n   484→        'n_neurons': n_final,\n   485→        'subject': subject_id,\n   486→        'scene': scene,\n   487→        'n_trials': len(neural_trials),\n   488→        'brain_region': location,\n   489→    }\n   490→\n   491→\n   492→def plot_processing(filepath, neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n   493→                   position, speed, lick_binary, trial_start_inds, teleport_inds,\n   494→                   rz_coords, rz_labels, trial_rewarded, timestamps,\n   495→                   subject_id, scene, session_idx):\n   496→    \"\"\"Plot processing visualizations for a session.\"\"\"\n   497→    import matplotlib\n   498→    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n   499→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n   500→\n   501→    fig, axes = plt.subplots(6, 2, figsize=(20, 24))\n   502→    fig.suptitle(f'{subject_id} - {scene}', fontsize=14)\n   503→\n   504→    # Pick 2 example trials\n   505→    for col, trial_idx in enumerate([5, min(35, len(neural_trials)-1)]):\n   506→        if trial_idx >= len(neural_trials):\n   507→            continue\n   508→\n   509→        neural = neural_trials[trial_idx]\n   510→        inp = input_trials[trial_idx]\n   511→        out = output_trials[trial_idx]\n   512→        n_tp = neural.shape[1]\n   513→        time_vec = inp[0, :]  # time from trial start\n   514→\n   515→        # Panel 1: Neural activity (mean across neurons)\n   516→        ax = axes[0, col]\n   517→        ax.plot(time_vec, np.mean(neural, axis=0), 'k', linewidth=0.5)\n   518→        ax.set_title(f'Trial {trial_idx}: Mean neural activity')\n   519→        ax.set_xlabel('Time (s)')\n   520→\n   521→        # Panel 2: Position and reward zone\n   522→        ax = axes[1, col]\n   523→        si = trial_start_inds[trial_idx]\n   524→        ei = teleport_inds[trial_idx]\n   525→        trial_pos = position[si:ei]\n   526→        ax.plot(time_vec[:len(trial_pos)], trial_pos, 'b')\n   527→        ax.axhspan(rz_coords[trial_idx, 0], rz_coords[trial_idx, 1], alpha=0.3, color='green')\n   528→        ax.set_title(f'Position (zone {rz_labels[trial_idx]})')\n   529→        ax.set_ylabel('Position (cm)')\n   530→\n   531→        # Panel 3: Distance to reward zone (discretized)\n   532→        ax = axes[2, col]\n   533→        ax.plot(time_vec, out[0, :], 'r')\n   534→        ax.set_title('Distance to reward zone (bins)')\n   535→        ax.set_ylabel('Bin')\n   536→\n   537→        # Panel 4: Speed (discretized)\n   538→        ax = axes[3, col]\n   539→        trial_speed = speed[si:ei]\n   540→        ax.plot(time_vec[:len(trial_speed)], trial_speed, 'b', alpha=0.5, label='raw')\n   541→        ax2 = ax.twinx()\n   542→        ax2.plot(time_vec, out[2, :], 'r', alpha=0.7, label='binned')\n   543→        ax.set_title('Speed')\n   544→        ax.set_ylabel('cm/s')\n   545→\n   546→        # Panel 5: Lick\n   547→        ax = axes[4, col]\n   548→        ax.plot(time_vec, out[3, :], 'k')\n   549→        ax.set_title('Lick (binary)')\n   550→\n   551→        # Panel 6: Inputs\n   552→        ax = axes[5, col]\n   553→        ax.text(0.1, 0.8, f'Env: {inp[1, 0]:.0f}', transform=ax.transAxes)\n   554→        ax.text(0.1, 0.6, f'Trial#: {inp[2, 0]:.0f}', transform=ax.transAxes)\n   555→        ax.text(0.1, 0.4, cm\nIMAGING_RATE_NOMINAL = 15.5078125  # Hz\n\ndef parse_args():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Convert NWB data to decoder format')\n    parser.add_argument('output', type=str, find_nwb_files(data_dir='data'):\n    \"\"\"Find all NWB files organized by subject.\"\"\"\n    subjects = sorted([d for d in os.listdir(data_dir) if d.startswith('sub-')])\n    sessions = []\n    for       'subject': subj.replace('sub-', ''),\n                'filepath': fpath,\n                'filename': os.path.basename(fpath),\n            })\n    return sessions\n\n\ndef parse_scene(identifier):\n    \"\"\"Parse scene name from NWB identifier to determine reward zones.\n\n    Returns:\n        scene: str, e.g. 'Env1_LocationB_to_A'\n    \"\"\"\n    return identifier.split('/')[-1]\n\n\ndef get_reward_zone_labels(scene, n_trials, change_trial=30):\n    \"\"\"Determine reward zone label (A/B/C) and environment for each trial based on scene name.\n\n    Scene formats:\n    - Single zone: Env1_LocationA, Env2_LocationB, etc.\n    - Within-env switch: Env1_LocationB_to_A, Env2_LocationA_to_C, etc.\n    - Cross-env switch: Env1_A_to_Env2_B, env_per_trial: (n_trials,) array of environment values (0=Env1, 1=Env2)\n    \"\"\"\n    labels = np.empty(n_trials, dtype='U1')\n    coords = np.zeros((n_trials, 2))\n    env_per_trial = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=np.int64)\n\n    if '_to_' in scene:\n        # Switch session - need to parse from and to\n        parts = scene.split('_')\n        to_idx = parts.index('to')\n\n        # Parse \"from\" side\n        from_parts = parts[:to_idx]\n        # Parse \"to\" side\n        to_parts = parts[to_idx+1:]\n\n        # Extract zone letter and env from each side\n        from_zone, from_env = _parse_zone_and_env(from_parts)\n        to_zone, to_env = _parse_zone_and_env(to_parts)\n\n        labels[:change_trial] = from_zone\n        labels[change_trial:] = to_zone\n        coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT[from_zone]\n        coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT[to_zone]\n        env_per_trial[:change_trial] = from_env\n        env_per_trial[change_trial:] = to_env\n    else:\n        # Single zone session: Env1_LocationA or similar\n        parts = scene.split('_')\n        zone, env = _parse_zone_and_env(parts)\n        labels[:] = zone\n        coords[:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT[zone]\n        env_per_trial[:] = env\n\n    return labels, coords, env_per_trial\n\n\ndef _parse_zone_and_env(parts):\n    \"\"\"Parse zone letter and env number from scene name parts.\n\n    Examples:\n        ['Env1', 'LocationA'] -> ('A', 0)\n        ['Env2', 'B'] -> ('B', 1)\n        ['Env1', 'LocationB'] -> ('B', 0)\n    \"\"\"\n    env = 0\n    zone = 'A'\n\n    for p in parts:\n        if p.startswith('Env'):\n            env_num = int(p.replace('Env', ''))\n            env = env_num - 1  # 0-indexed\n        elif p.startswith('Location'):\n            zone = p.replace('Location', '')\n        elif len(p) == 1 and p in 'ABC':\n            zone = p\n\n    return zone, env\n\n\ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, rz_start, rz_end):\n    \"\"\"Compute signed distance from position to reward zone.\n\n    Negative = before zone (position < zone start)\n    Positive = after zone (position > zone end)\n    Zero = inside zone\n    \"\"\"\n    dist = np.zeros_like(position)\n    before = position < rz_start\n    after = position > rz_end\n    inside = ~before & ~after\n\n    dist[before] = position[before] - rz_start  # negative\n    dist[after] = position[after] - rz_end       # positive\n    dist[inside] = 0.0\n\n    return dist\n\n\ndef discretize_distance(distance):\n    \"\"\"Discretize distance to reward zone into 7 bins.\n    0: < -50 cm\n    1: -50 to -10 cm\n    2: -10 cm to < 0 cm\n    3: 0 cm (in zone)\n    4: >0 cm to +10 cm\n    5: +10 to +50 cm\n    6: > +50 cm\n    \"\"\"\n    bins = np.zeros(len(distance), dtype=np.int64)\n    bins[distance < -50] = 0\n    bins[(distance >= -50) & (distance < -10)] = 1\n    bins[(distance >= -10) & (distance < 0)] = 2\n    bins[distance == 0] = 3\n    bins[(distance > 0) & (distance <= 10)] = 4\n    bins[(distance > 10) & (distance <= 50)] = 5\n    bins[distance > 50] = 6\n    return bins\n\n\ndef discretize_position(position):\n    \"\"\"Discretize absolute position into 5 equal bins (0-90, 90-180, 180-270, 270-360, 360-450).\"\"\"\n    bins = np.clip(np.floor(position / 90.0).astype(np.int64), 0, 4)\n    return bins\n\n\ndef discretize_speed(speed):\n    \"\"\"Discretize speed into 5 bins.\n    0: < 2 cm/s\n    1: 2-10 cm/s\n    2: 10-20 cm/s\n    3: 20-40 cm/s\n    4: > 40 cm/s\n    \"\"\"\n    bins = np.zeros(len(speed), dtype=np.int64)\n    bins[speed < 2] = 0\n    bins[(speed >= 2) & (speed < 10)] = 1\n    bins[(speed >= 10) & (speed < 20)] = 2\n    bins[(speed >= 20) & (speed < 40)] = 3\n    bins[speed >= 40] = 4\n    return bins\n\n\ndef process_session(filepath, show_processing=False, session_idx=0):\n    \"\"\"Process a single NWB file and return trial-segmented data.\n\n    Returns dict with keys: neural, input,  # Determine available planes\n        deconv_group = f['processing/ophys/Deconvolved']\n        planes = sorted(deconv_group.keys())  # e.g. ['plane0'] or ['plane0', 'plane1']\n\n        # Load and concatenate data from all planes\n        deconv_list = []\n        flu_list = []\n        neu_list = []\n        plane_cell_offset = 0\n        plane_offsets = {}\n        for plane in planes:\n            plane_num = int(plane.replace('plane', ''))\n            plane_mask = planeIdx == plane_num\n            plane_offsets[plane_num] = np.where(plane_mask)[0]\n\n            d = f[f'processing/ophys/Deconvolved/{plane}/data'][:]  # (n_timepoints, n_rois_plane)\n            fl = f[f'processing/ophys/Fluorescence/{plane}/data'][:]\n            ne = f[f'processing/ophys/Neuropil/{plane}/data'][:]\n            deconv_list.append(d)\n            flu_list.append(fl)\n            neu_list.append(ne)\n\n        # Concatenate across planes: (n_timepoints, n_rois_total_in_data)\n        deconv = np.concatenate(deconv_list, axis=1)\n        fluorescence = np.concatenate(flu_list, axis=1)\n        neuropil_data = np.concatenate(neu_list, axis=1)\n\n        # Build mapping from concatenated column index to iscell row index\n        concat_to_iscell = []\n        for plane in planes:\n            plane_num = int(plane.replace('plane', ''))\n            concat_to_iscell.append(plane_offsets[plane_num])\n        concat_to_iscell = np.concatenate(concat_to_iscell)\n\n        # === Behavior ===\n        position = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/position/data'][:]\n        speed = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/speed/data'][:]\n        lick = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/lick/data'][:]\n        environment = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/environment/data'][:]\n        trial_start = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/trial_start/data'][:]\n        teleport_sig = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/teleport/data'][:]\n        behav_timestamps = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/position/timestamps'][:]\n\n        # Reward events (sparse)\n        reward_timestamps = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/Reward/timestamps'][:]\n\n    n_timepoints, n_rois_concat = deconv.shape\n\n    # === Align behavior and neural data lengths ===\n    n_behav = len(position)\n    if n_timepoints != n_behav:\n        min_len = min(n_timepoints, n_behav)\n        print(f\"  NOTE: Aligned neural ({n_timepoints}) and behavior ({n_behav}) to {min_len} speed[:min_len]\n        lick = lick[:min_len]\n        environment = environment[:min_len]\n        trial_start = trial_start[:min_len]\n        teleport_sig = teleport_sig[:min_len]\n        behav_timestamps = behav_timestamps[:min_len]\n        n_timepoints = min_len\n\n    # === Cell filtering ===\n    # Step 1: iscell filter - map to concatenated indices\n    cell_mask_concat = neuropil_data\n    f_median = np.median(f_corrected, axis=0, keepdims=True)\n    f_median[f_median == 0] = 1\n    dff_simple = (f_corrected - f_median) / np.abs(f_median)\n\n    valid_mask = (speed > 0) & (position >= 0) & ~np.isnan(speed)\n    interneuron_mask = np.zeros(n_rois_concat, dtype=bool)\n\n    if valid_mask.sum() > 100:\n        speed_valid = speed[valid_mask]\n        accepted_cols = np.where(cell_mask_concat)[0]\n        if len(accepted_cols) > 0:\n            # Vectorized correlation: z-score speed and dff, then dot product\n            speed_z = (speed_valid - speed_valid.mean()) / (speed_valid.std() + 1e-10)\n            dff_accepted = dff_simple[valid_mask][:, accepted_cols]\n            dff_mean = dff_accepted.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)\n            dff_std = dff_accepted.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)\n            dff_std[dff_std == 0] = 1e-10\n            dff_z = (dff_accepted - dff_mean) / dff_std\n            corrs = (speed_z @ dff_z) / len(speed_z)\n            for i, col_idx in enumerate(accepted_cols):\n                if corrs[i] > 0.5:\n                    interneuron_mask[col_idx] = True\n\n    # Final cell mask\n    final_cell_mask = cell_mask_concat & ~interneuron_mask\n    n_accepted = cell_mask_concat.sum()\n    n_interneurons = (cell_mask_concat & interneuron_mask).sum()\n    n_final = final_cell_mask.sum()\n\n    # Filter neural data to accepted cells\n    neural_all = deconv[:, final_cell_mask].T  # (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\n    neural_all = neural_all.astype(np.float32)\n\n    # === Trial boundaries ===\n    trial_start_inds = np.where(trial_start > 0)[0]\n    teleport_inds = np.where(teleport_sig > 0)[0]\n    n_trials = len(trial_start_inds)\n\n    if n_trials < 2:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: Only {n_trials} trials, skipping session\")\n        return None\n\n    # Match teleport to trial start (ensure same count)\n    if len(teleport_inds) != n_trials:\n        # Try to match: each teleport should come after corresponding trial start\n        matched_teleports = []\n        for ts in trial_start_inds:\n            tp_after = teleport_inds[teleport_inds > ts]\n            if len(tp_after) > 0:\n                matched_teleports.append(tp_after[0])\n        teleport_inds = np.array(matched_teleports)\n        n_trials = min(n_trials, len(teleport_inds))\n        trial_start_inds = trial_start_inds[:n_trials]\n\n    # === Reward zone labels ===\n    rz_labels, rz_coords, env_per_trial = get_reward_zone_labels(scene, n_trials)\n\n    # === Map reward events to np.argmin(np.abs(behav_timestamps - rt))\n        reward_frames[frame_idx] = 1.0\n\n    # Determine reward outcome per trial\n    trial_rewarded = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=np.int64)\n    for t in # Lick sensor error correction per trial (>30% frames with count>2 -> NaN)\n    for t in range(n_trials):\n        si = trial_start_inds[t]\n        ei = teleport_inds[t]\n        trial_lick = lick[si:ei]\n        if len(trial_lick) > 0 and (np.sum(trial_lick > 2) / len(trial_lick)) > 0.30:\n            lick_binary[si:ei] = 0  # Set to 0 instead of NaN for cleaner output\n\n    # === Previous trial outcome (lagged) ===\n    prev_outcome = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=np.int64)\n    for t in range(1, n_trials):\n        prev_outcome[t] = trial_rewarded[t - 1]\n    # Trial 0: no previous, use 0 (omission)\n\n    # === Segment into trials ===\n    neural_trials = []\n    input_trials = []\n    output_trials = []\n\n    frame_time = 1.0 / imaging_rate  # seconds per frame\n\n    for t in range(n_trials):\n        si = trial_start_inds[t]\n        ei = teleport_inds[t]\n        n_tp = ei - si\n\n        if n_tp < 2:\n            continue\n\n        # Neural: (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\n        trial_neural = neural_all[:, si:ei].copy()\n        # Replace NaN with 0 in neural data\n        trial_neural = np.nan_to_num(trial_neural, nan=0.0)\n\n        # === Inputs ===\n        # Time from start of trial (seconds)\n        time_from_start = np.arange(n_tp, dtype=np.float32) * frame_time\n\n        # Per-trial inputs\n        env_type = np.float32(trial_env[t])\n        trial_num = np.float32(t)\n        prev_out = np.float32(prev_outcome[t])\n\n        # input shape: (n_input, n_timepoints) for time-varying, or (n_input,) for per-trial\n        # We have 1 time-varying + 3 per-trial\n        # Time-varying: time_from_start (1, n_tp)\n        # Per-trial: env_type, trial_num, prev_outcome (3,)\n        trial_input_tv = time_from_start.reshape(1, -1)  # (1, n_tp)\n        trial_input_pt = np.array([env_type, trial_num, prev_out], dtype=np.float32)  # (3,)\n        # Combine: first dim is time-varying, rest are per-trial\n        # Per spec: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input,)\n        # We need to combine: expand per-trial to time-varying by repeating\n        trial_input = np.vstack([\n            trial_input_tv,\n            np.full((1, n_tp), env_type, dtype=np.float32),\n            np.full((1, n_tp), trial_num, dtype=np.float32),\n            np.full((1, n_tp), prev_out, dtype=np.float32),\n        ])  # (4, n_tp)\n\n        # === Outputs ===\n        trial_pos = position[si:ei]\n        trial_speed = speed[si:ei]\n        trial_lick = lick_binary[si:ei]\n\n        # Distance to reward zone\n        rz_start = rz_coords[t, 0]\n        rz_end = rz_coords[t, 1]\n        signed_dist = compute_distance_to_reward_zone(trial_pos, rz_start, rz_end)\n        dist_bins = discretize_distance(signed_dist)\n\n        # Absolute position bins\n        pos_bins = discretize_position(trial_pos)\n\n        # Speed bins\n        speed_bins = discretize_speed(np.abs(trial_speed))  # use absolute speed\n\n        # Lick binary\n        lick_out = (trial_lick > 0).astype(np.int64)\n\n        # Reward zone location (per-trial): A=0, B=1, C=2\n        zone_map = {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'C': 2}\n        rz_loc = zone_map.get(rz_labels[t], 0)\n\n        # Reward outcome (per-trial): 0=no, 1=yes\n        rew_outcome = int(trial_rewarded[t])\n\n        # Combine outputs: time-varying + per-trial\n        # Time-varying: dist_bins, pos_bins, speed_bins, lick (4, n_tp)\n        # Per-trial: rz_loc, rew_outcome (2,)\n        trial_output = np.vstack([\n            dist_bins.reshape(1, -1),\n            pos_bins.reshape(1, -1),\n            speed_bins.reshape(1, -1),\n            lick_out.reshape(1, -1),\n            np.full((1, n_tp), rz_loc, dtype=np.int64),\n            np.full((1, n_tp), rew_outcome, dtype=np.int64),\n        ])  # (6, n_tp)\n\n        neural_trials.append(trial_neural.astype(np.float32))\n        input_trials.append(trial_input.astype(np.float32))\n        output_trials.append(trial_output.astype(np.int64))\n\n    t1 = time.time()\n    print(f\"  {os.path.basename(filepath)}: {n_final} neurons ({n_accepted} accepted, {n_interneurons} interneurons removed), \"\n          f\"{len(neural_trials)} trials, scene={scene}, env={np.unique(trial_env)}, \"\n          f\"reward_rate={trial_rewarded.mean():.2f}, time={t1-t0:.1f}s\")\n\n    if show_processing and session_idx < 2:\n        plot_processing(filepath, neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n                       position, speed, lick_binary, trial_start_inds, teleport_inds,\n                       rz_coords, rz_labels, trial_rewarded, behav_timestamps,\n                       subject_id, scene, session_idx)\n\n    return {\n        'neural': neural_trials,\n        'input': input_trials,\n        'output': output_trials,\n        'n_neurons': n_final,\n        'subject': subject_id,\n        'scene': scene,\n        'n_trials': len(neural_trials),\n        'brain_region': location,\n    }\n\n\ndef plot_processing(filepath, neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n                   position, speed, lick_binary, Dataset Conversion Notes\n\n## Overview\n- **Dataset**: Sosa, Plitt, Giocomo 2025 - \"A flexible hippocampal population code for experience relative to reward\"\n- **Date started**: 2026-03-10\n- **Goal**: Convert to decoder-compatible format\n- **Data source**: DANDI:001361 NWB files, 2P calcium imaging of CA1\n\n## Step 0: Setup and Initialization\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\nDirectory contents:\n- `code/` - Reference code (notebooks, src/reward_relative/, docs/)\n- `data/` - NWB files by subject (sub-m3, m4, m7, m11-m15, m17-m19) = 11 subjects, 152 sessions\n- `paper.pdf`, Identified\n| Function | File | Stage | Purpose |\n|----------|------|-------|---------|\n| `dff()` | preprocessing.py | PROCESSING | Compute deltaF/F with maximin baseline, neuropil subtraction, deconvolution |\n| `multi_anim_sess()` | utilities.py | LOADING+PROCESSING | Load sess, compute dFF, events, place cells for multiple animals |\n| `get_trial_types()` | behavior.py | LOADING | Get isreward, morph (env) per trial |\n| `get_reward_zones()` | behavior.py | LOADING | Get reward zone coords and labels (A/B/C) per trial |\n| `get_timeseries_data()` | glmUtils.py | PROCESSING | cumcount>2, set to NaN |\n| `CircularRegression` | decode.py | DECODING | Circular-linear regression for RR position decoding |\n| `train_vs_test_blocks()` | decode.py | DECODING | Cross-validated decoder training |\n\n### Notes\n- **Neural data flow**: Raw F -> neuropil subtraction (coef=0.7) -> maximin baseline dF/F (20s window) -> smooth (2 sample Gaussian) -> deconvolution (OASIS) -> \"events\"\n- **NWB files already contain**: Fluorescence (raw F), Neuropil (Fneu), Deconvolved events, iscell\n- **Key**: NWB `Deconvolved` data is the deconvolved events AFTER full dF/F processing pipeline (computed from the multi_anim_sess notebook)\n- **iscell**: Suite2p cell curation stored in NWB; iscell[:,0]==1 means accepted cell\n- **Behavior aligned to imaging**: All behavior timeseries at ~15.5 Hz imaging frame rate\n- **Trial boundaries**: trial_start and teleport signals in behavior timeseries\n- **Speed threshold**: Activity excluded when speed < 2 cm/s for place cell analyses and the decoder in the paper\n- **The paper's decoder** decodes circular reward-relative position, NOT the variables we need for our decoder task\n\n### dF/F Processing (from preprocessing.py):\n1. NaN out inter-trial (teleport) periods\n2. Neuropil subtraction: F = F - 0.7*Fneu (then add back mean Fneu per trial)\n3. Maximin baseline: smooth F with sigma=15, min filter 300 samples (~20s), max filter 300 samples\n4. dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n5. Smooth dF/F with 2-sample Gaussian per trial\n6. Deconvolve with OASIS (tau=0.7, frame_rate ~15.5 Hz)\n\n**Important**: The NWB files contain the ALREADY PROCESSED deconvolved events. We should use these directly.\n\n---\n\n## Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Data Structure\nNWB files organized: `data/sub-{id}/sub-{id}_ses-{ses}_behavior+ophys.nwb`\n\nEach NWB contains:\n- `processing/ophys/Fluorescence/plane0/data` - (n_timepoints, n_ROIs) raw fluorescence\n- `processing/ophys/Neuropil/plane0/data` - (n_timepoints, n_ROIs) neuropil\n- `processing/ophys/Deconvolved/plane0/data` - (n_timepoints, n_ROIs) deconvolved events\n- `processing/ophys/ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/iscell` - (n_ROIs, 2) cell selection\n- `processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/` with: position, speed, lick, Reward (event times), environment, trial_start, teleport, trial number, reward_zone, scanning, autoreward\n- `general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/imaging_rate` - ~15.5 Hz\n- `general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/location` - \"hippocampus, CA1\"\n- `identifier` - contains scene info (e.g., Env1_LocationB_to_A)\n\n### Dataset Size (from data files)\n| Statistic | Value |\n|-----------|-------|\n| Subjects | 11 (m3, m4, m7, m11-m15, m17-m19) |\n| Sessions total | 152 |\n| Sessions/subject | 12-14 (m11 has 12, rest 14) |\n| Neurons/session | 155-2339 accepted (from iscell + interneuron exclusion) |\n| Trials/session | ~80 (mean 80.4 +/- 6.1) |\n| Imaging rate | ~15.5 Hz (~64.5 ms/frame) |\n| Track length | 450 cm |\n\n### Multi-plane Data\n- Subjects m17 and m18 have 2-plane imaging (plane0 + plane1)\n- ~15.5 Hz per plane, data concatenated across planes\n- Off-by-one behavior/neural length mismatch in some sessions (truncated to shorter)\n\n### NWB Reward field\n- `Reward/data` and `Reward/timestamps`: Event-based (sparse), not frame-aligned. [-np.inf,50,150,250,350,np.inf]\n    21→speed_bins = [-np.inf, 2, 10, 20, 40, np.inf]\n    22→output_values = [\n    23→    [\n    24→        '< -50 cm',\n    25→        '-50 to -10 cm',\n    26→        '-10 cm to < 0 cm',\n    27→        '0 cm',\n    28→        '>0 cm to +10 cm',\n    29→        '+10 to +50 cm',\n    30→        '> +50 cm'\n    31→    ],\n    32→    [\n    33→        '-50 to 50 cm',\n    34→        '50 to 150 cm',\n    35→        '150 to 250 cm',\n    36→        '250 to 350 cm',\n    37→        '350 to 450 cm',\n    38→    ],\n    39→    [\n    40→        '< 2 cm/s',\n    41→        '2 to 10 cm/s',\n    42→        '10 to 20 cm/s',\n    43→        '20 to 40 cm/s',\n    44→        '> 40 cm/s',\n    45→    ],\n    46→    ['no', 'yes'],\n    47→    ['A', 'B', 'C'],\n    48→    ['no','yes']\n    49→]\n    50→\n    51→# inspired by Sosa_et_al_2024/src/reward_relative/behavior.py\n    52→reward_zone_dict = {'A': [80, 130],\n    53→                    'B': [200, 250],\n    54→                    'C': [320, 370]}\n    55→\n    56→\n    57→def get_subjects(datadir=DATADIR):\n    58→    # find all directories in datadir that start with 'sub-'\n    59→    subjects = [d.split('-')[1] for d in os.listdir(datadir) if d.startswith('sub-')]\n    60→    return subjects\n    61→\n    62→def get_sessions(subject, datadir=DATADIR):\n    63→    subject_dir = os.path.join(datadir, f\"sub-{subject}\")\n    64→    # parse sessions from files named sub-<subject>-<session>_behavior+ophys.nwb\n    65→    nwb_files = [f for f in os.listdir(subject_dir) if f.endswith('.nwb')]\n    66→    sessions = [int(f.split('_')[1].split('-')[1]) for f in nwb_files]\n    67→    return sessions\n    68→\n    69→def get_nwb_file(subject, session, datadir=DATADIR):\n    70→    nwb_file_path = os.path.join(datadir, f\"sub-{subject}\", f\"sub-{subject}_ses-{session:02d}_behavior+ophys.nwb\")\n    71→    assert os.path.exists(nwb_file_path), f\"File {nwb_file_path} does not exist\"\n    72→    return nwb_file_path\n    73→\n    74→def interp(new_idx,old_idx,old_data,**kwargs):\n    75→    f = interp1d(old_idx, old_data, axis=0, **kwargs)\n    76→    return f(new_idx)\n    77→\n    78→\n    79→def get_trial_starts_ends(trial_start,teleport):\n    80→    trial_start_idx = np.nonzero(trial_start)[0]\n    81→    is_teleport_start = (teleport[1:] > 0) & (teleport[:-1] <= 0)\n    82→    trial_end_idx = np.nonzero(is_teleport_start)[0] + 1 \n    83→    assert len(trial_start_idx) == len(trial_end_idx), f\"Number of trial starts ({len(trial_start_idx)}) does not match number of trial ends ({len(trial_end_idx)})\"\n    84→    return trial_start_idx, trial_end_idx\n    85→        \n    86→\n    87→def survey_session_data(subject, session, datadir=DATADIR):\n    88→    nwb_file_path = get_nwb_file(subject, session, datadir)\n    89→    info = {}\n    90→    info['reward_zone_positions'] = []\n    91→    with pynwb.NWBHDF5IO(nwb_file_path, 'r') as io:\n    92→        nwbfile = io.read()\n    93→        # parse neural activity data\n    94→        deconvolved_data = nwbfile.processing[\"ophys\"][\"Deconvolved\"]\n    95→        spike_data = []\n    96→        nplanes = len(deconvolved_data.roi_response_series)\n    97→        for plane_data in deconvolved_data.roi_response_series.values():\n    98→            roi_data = plane_data.rois[:]\n    99→            iscell = np.array(roi_data['iscell'].to_list())[:,0] == 1\n   100→            spike_data_curr = plane_data.data[:]\n   101→            assert spike_data_curr.shape[1] == iscell.shape[0], f\"Number of neurons in data ({spike_data_curr.shape[1]}) does not match number of iscell entries ({iscell.shape[0]})\"\n   102→            spike_data.append(spike_data_curr[:,iscell])\n   103→        spike_data = np.concatenate(spike_data, axis=1)\n   104→        info['T'], info['nneurons'] = spike_data.shape\n   105→        max_spike_rate = np.max(spike_data)\n   106→        info['max_spike_rate_range'] = (max_spike_rate.min(), max_spike_rate.max())\n   107→        info['neural_time_bin_size'] = nplanes/plane_data.rate*1000 # ms\n   108→\n   109→        behavior_data = nwbfile.processing['behavior']['BehavioralTimeSeries'].time_series       \n   110→        istrial = behavior_data['trial number'].data[:] >= 0\n   111→        # reward_amounts are all the same\n   112→        reward_amounts = behavior_data['Reward'].data[:]\n   113→        info['unique_reward_amounts'] = np.unique(reward_amounts)\n   114→        info['nrewards'] = len(reward_amounts)\n   115→        # autoreward is all 0s, so not useful for modeling, but check that it is all zeros\n   116→        autoreward = behavior_data['autoreward'].data[:]\n   117→        info['unique_autoreward'] = np.unique(autoreward[istrial])\n   118→        # vr environment\n   119→        vr_environment = behavior_data['environment'].data[:] # one value per time point, does this change by session?\n   120→        info['unique_vr_environment'] = np.unique(vr_environment[istrial])\n   121→        licks = behavior_data['lick'].data[:]\n   122→        info['unique_licks'] = np.unique(licks[istrial])\n   123→        position = behavior_data['position'].data[:]\n   124→        info['position_range'] = (position[istrial].min(), position[istrial].max())\n   125→        reward_zone = behavior_data['reward_zone'].data[:]\n   126→        info['unique_reward_zone'] = np.unique(reward_zone[istrial])\n   127→        scanning = behavior_data['scanning'].data[:]\n   128→        info['unique_scanning'] = np.unique(scanning[istrial])\n   129→        speed = behavior_data['speed'].data[:]\n   130→        info['speed_range'] = (speed[istrial].min(), speed[istrial].max())\n   131→        teleport = behavior_data['teleport'].data[:]\n   132→        info['unique_teleport'] = np.unique(teleport[istrial])\n   133→        \n   134→        # trial number and trial start do not agree with each other\n   135→        # use trial_start and teleport to find starts and ends of trials\n   136→        trial_start_idx, trial_end_idx = get_trial_starts_ends(behavior_data['trial_start'].data[:], teleport)\n   137→        trial_length = trial_end_idx - trial_start_idx\n   138→        info['trial_length_range'] = (trial_length.min(), trial_length.max())\n   139→\n   140→        ntrials = len(trial_start_idx)\n   141→        info['ntrials'] = ntrials\n   142→        info['reward_zone_positions'] = np.full((ntrials, 2), np.nan)\n   143→        for trial in range(ntrials):\n   144→            idx = np.arange(trial_start_idx[trial], trial_end_idx[trial], dtype=int)\n   145→            pos_trial = position[idx]\n   146→            reward_zone_trial = reward_zone[idx] > 0\n   147→            if not np.any(reward_zone_trial):\n   148→                continue\n   149→            info['reward_zone_positions'][trial] = [np.min(pos_trial[reward_zone_trial]), np.max(pos_trial[reward_zone_trial])]\n   150→                    \n   151→    return info\n   152→    \n   153→def get_bin_centers(bin_edges,data):\n   154→    # replace infinity with the max/min of the data for plotting purposes\n   155→    bin_edges_plot = np.array(bin_edges.copy())\n   156→    if np.isinf(bin_edges_plot[0]):\n   157→        bin_edges_plot[0] = data.min()\n   158→        if bin_edges_plot[0] > bin_edges_plot[1]:\n   159→            bin_edges_plot[0] = 2*bin_edges_plot[1] - bin_edges_plot[2] # extrapolate to get a lower value for the first bin edge\n   160→    if np.isinf(bin_edges_plot[-1]):\n   161→        bin_edges_plot[-1] = data.max()\n   162→        if bin_edges_plot[-1] < bin_edges_plot[-2]:\n   163→            bin_edges_plot[-1] = 2*bin_edges_plot[-2] - bin_edges_plot[-3] # extrapolate to get a higher value for the last bin edge\n   164→    bin_centers = (bin_edges_plot[:-1] + bin_edges_plot[1:]) / 2\n   165→    return bin_centers\n   166→    \n   167→def compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position_curr, reward_zone_curr):\n   168→    ntimepoints = len(position_curr)\n   169→    reward_zone_range = reward_zone_dict[reward_zone_curr]\n   170→    distance_to_reward_zone = np.zeros(ntimepoints)\n   171→    idx_before_zone = position_curr < reward_zone_range[0]\n   172→    distance_to_reward_zone[idx_before_zone] = position_curr[idx_before_zone] - reward_zone_range[0] # negative if before the zone\n   173→    idx_after_zone = position_curr > reward_zone_range[1]\n   174→    distance_to_reward_zone[idx_after_zone] = position_curr[idx_after_zone] - reward_zone_range[1] # positive if after the zone\n   175→    return distance_to_reward_zone\n   176→    \n   177→def convert_session_data(subject, session, info, trial_offset, time_bin_size=None, datadir=DATADIR, \n   178→                         show_processing=False, max_neurons_plot=30, min_ntimepoints=50,\n   179→                         figfile=None):\n   180→\n   181→    nwb_file_path = get_nwb_file(subject, session, datadir)\n   182→    io = pynwb.NWBHDF5IO(nwb_file_path, 'r')\n   183→    nwbfile = io.read()\n   184→\n   185→    # parse neural activity data\n   186→    deconvolved_data = nwbfile.processing[\"ophys\"][\"Deconvolved\"]\n   187→    spike_data = []\n   188→    nplanes = len(deconvolved_data.roi_response_series)\n   189→    for plane_data in deconvolved_data.roi_response_series.values():\n   190→        roi_data = plane_data.rois[:]\n   191→        iscell = np.array(roi_data['iscell'].to_list())[:,0] == 1\n   192→        spike_data_curr = plane_data.data[:]\n   193→        assert spike_data_curr.shape[1] == iscell.shape[0], f\"Number of neurons in data ({spike_data_curr.shape[1]}) does not match number of iscell entries ({iscell.shape[0]})\"\n   194→        spike_data.append(spike_data_curr[:,iscell])\n   195→    spike_data = np.concatenate(spike_data, axis=1)\n   196→    T = spike_data.shape[0]\n   197→    neural_time_bin_size = nplanes/plane_data.rate*1000 # ms\n   198→    \n   199→    behavior_data = nwbfile.processing['behavior']['BehavioralTimeSeries'].time_series\n   200→    \n   201→    timestamps = behavior_data['trial number'].timestamps[:] \n   202→    if T != len(timestamps):\n   203→        print(f\"Warning: number of neural time bins ({T}) does not match number of behavior timestamps ({len(timestamps)}), cropping to the minimum of the two\")\n   204→        T = min(T, len(timestamps))\n   205→        spike_data = spike_data[:T]\n   206→        timestamps = timestamps[:T]\n   207→    \n   208→    behavior_time_bin_size = np.median(np.diff(timestamps))*1000 # ms\n   209→    \n   210→    # trial number for each time point\n   211→    trial_start_idx, trial_end_idx = get_trial_starts_ends(behavior_data['trial_start'].data[:], behavior_data['teleport'].data[:])\n   212→    ntrials = len(trial_start_idx)\n   213→    \n   214→    # reward_amounts are all the same\n   215→    reward_amounts = behavior_data['Reward'].data[:][:T]\n   216→    assert np.allclose(reward_amounts,reward_amounts[0]), f\"Reward amounts are not all the same, unique values: {np.unique(reward_amounts)}\"\n   217→    reward_times = behavior_data['Reward'].timestamps[:] # in seconds        \n   218→    # find the closest timestamp to reward times, not careful for mismatches right now\n   219→    reward_time_indices = np.searchsorted(timestamps, reward_times)\n   220→    err = timestamps[reward_time_indices] - reward_times\n   221→    assert np.all(np.abs(err) <= time_bin_size/2), f\"Reward times do not align with neural time bins, max error: {np.max(np.abs(err))} s\"\n   222→    isreward = np.zeros(T)\n   223→    isreward[reward_time_indices] = 1\n   224→\n   225→    # autoreward is all 0s, so not useful for modeling, but check that it is all zeros\n   226→\n   227→    # vr environment\n   228→    vr_environment = behavior_data['environment'].data[:][:T] # one value per time point, does this change by session?\n   229→    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['environment'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for environment do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   230→\n   231→    # lick\n   232→    licks = behavior_data['lick'].data[:][:T]\n   233→    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['lick'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for lick do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   234→\n   235→    # current position in the VR environment\n   236→    position = behavior_data['position'].data[:][:T]\n   237→    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['position'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for position do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   238→\n   239→    # teleport -- not sure what to do with this, maybe should end the trial early??\n   240→    #isteleport = behavior_data['teleport'].data[:][:T] == 1\n   241→    #assert np.allclose(behavior_data['teleport'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for teleport do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   242→\n   243→    # reward zone -- take from pre-segmented data in info\n   244→    reward_zone = info['reward_zone_labels'][subject][trial_offset:trial_offset+ntrials]\n   245→\n   246→    # scanning is all 1s, so not useful for modeling\n   247→    \n   248→    # speed\n   249→    speed = behavior_data['speed'].data[:][:T] # cm / s\n   250→    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['speed'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for speed do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   251→\n   252→    io.close()\n   253→\n   254→    assert len(timestamps) == T, f\"Number of timestamps ({len(timestamps)}) does not match number of neural time bins ({T})\"\n   255→    if time_bin_size is not None:\n   256→        assert np.isclose(neural_time_bin_size, time_bin_size, rtol=1e-2), f\"Neural time bin size ({neural_time_bin_size} ms) does not match specified time bin size ({time_bin_size} ms)\"\n   257→        assert np.isclose(behavior_time_bin_size, time_bin_size, rtol=1e-2), f\"Behavior time bin size ({behavior_time_bin_size} ms) does not match specified time bin size ({time_bin_size} ms)\"\n   258→    \n   259→    neural = []\n   260→    input = []\n   261→    output = []\n   262→    ninputs = len(input_names)\n   263→    noutputs = len(output_names)\n   264→    istrial = np.ones(ntrials,dtype=bool) # keep track of whether we have dropped a trial for having too few time points\n   265→    min_distance_to_reward_zone = np.inf\n   266→    max_distance_to_reward_zone = -np.inf\n   267→    for trial in range(ntrials):\n   268→        idx = np.arange(trial_start_idx[trial], trial_end_idx[trial], dtype=int)\n   269→        if idx.sum() < min_ntimepoints:\n   270→            print(f\"Skipping trial {trial} in subject {subject} session {session} because it has only {idx.sum()} < {min_ntimepoints} time points\")\n   271→            istrial[trial] = False\n   272→            continue\n   273→\n   274→        spike_data_curr = spike_data[idx]\n   275→        timestamps_curr = timestamps[idx]\n   276→        vr_environment_curr =
